**Class.** Every row that renders a price, an equivalent, a cutoff, a cookie, a redirect, a facet, a migration or consent text keeps the breaker (DoD §4). The trust bar row (AC-114 to AC-116) renders consumer-facing claims (UCPD Art. 6) and reads the payments flag, so it keeps the breaker too.

#### Open questions (each default binds until the founder answers; the orchestrator applies them)

- **Q-24a. Which products are the core range?** The model is decided (founder, 2026-10-24: fixed, curated, 8 to 12, florists commit, vendor links check). **Default:** the catalogue research §5 rows 1 to 10, each mapped by the orchestrator to the committed SKU closest in type, main flower and colour, listed in the `/plan-tasks` PR for the founder to confirm there; no row that renders the core range merges before he confirms.
- **Q-24b. Picker buttons.** **Default: 6 by the admin's `sortWeight`, then the searchable field.** Alternative: every shop country as a button until 12.
- **Q-24c. The sentence picker leaves the home.** **Default: yes**, replaced by the country picker; its who and occasion parts live on as the tabs and "For whom"; A-founder-2026-10-21d's listbox lapses with it. Alternative: keep it under the picker (a longer first screen; the first product moves down about 200 px).
- **Q-24d. "Send flowers" leaves the desktop header.** **Default: yes** (the picker is on the home's first screen and the selector is in every header).
- **Q-24e. The product page's preselected size** when reached through the chooser. **Default: unchanged** (spec 009's default size, schema price unchanged).
- **Q-24f. Equivalents' display bound.** **Default: 14 days.** Alternative: 7 days, which may hide the line in a quiet week until TASK-071's daily refresh.
- **Q-24g. If the script budget cannot hold every deferred addition.** **Default: drop the hover first** (click, tap and keyboard still open the menu), then ask before raising A1's ceiling. The chooser's history replace and the price fill are never dropped.
- **Q-24h. "Only in {country}" on local specials.** **Default: yes.**
- **Q-24i. Go-live with a core product no florist makes in the new country.** **Default: the preflight item fails and the typed code may override it, audited;** the product then leaves every country-less page until a florist there covers it.
- **Q-24j. Waitlist double opt-in.** **Default: yes** (German practice for any email; one extra click).
- **Q-24k. Hubs with few core products.** With 8 to 12 core products, a hub may show only a few cards before a choice (founder: florists' own bouquets never show before a country is chosen). **Default:** the hub keeps its existence rule (spec 008 AC-70), shows every core product it carries plus the picker, and spec 008 A-founder-2026-10-21c AC-72's "at least 6 cards" is read on the hub's country pages; the thin-hub risk is checked by `/seo-audit` before the indexing flip. Alternative: a larger core range (toward the research's 20).
- **Q-24l. How long `fo_dest` is kept.** **Default: 180 days, with `shop.choose.remember` at the point of choice** (WP194's "clear indication" for a preference kept beyond the session); the lawyer reads the basis with the legal set (`plan/13` B4). Alternative: 30 days.
- **Answered:** the trust bar is option 2, three points by the buy button and one row in the home's hero card (founder "2", 2026-10-24, item 11); the price before a country is option B (founder "b", 2026-10-24); "our florist" stays (founder "trust", 2026-10-24): no copy changes it; repetition falls only because the example label shows once per grid, with its text unchanged.

### A-orchestrator-2026-11-01a: No em dash in any user-facing text (orchestrator ruling under the founder's delegation; new AC-117, T-123)

- **Ruling.** The orchestrator's, 2026-11-01, under the founder's blanket delegation of 2026-10-31 (spec 003 §14 A-founder-2026-10-31a, "you have my approval, just do it"). It applies the founder's standing rule of 2026-10-05, "approve the wording just no em dash" (spec 003 §14 A-founder-2026-10-15a), to body copy. These are not the founder's words on this amendment; the founder can take it back in one word.
- **Trigger.** TASK-184 (PR 432, 2026-10-25) found em dashes still live in `en` body copy that no spec clause owns. Spec 003 A-founder-2026-10-15a covers only `<title>`, meta descriptions and structured-data names, and its clause 9 left body copy to the copy batches; these lines were never in one. On main `fc74ec5d3`, 14 `en` keys hold U+2014. One is TASK-428's (`company.support.hours`, round 2 P6) and four are TASK-251's (`consent.essentialLocked` and the three `consent.cookies.*.purpose`). Nine have no owner: `corridor.facts.orderBy.value`, `corridor.facts.deliveryDays.none`, `corridor.facts.soonest.none`, `corridor.facts.cities.none`, `corridor.facts.prices.none`, `corridor.steps.guide.oneBody`, `corridor.calendar.intro`, `occasionHub.dateUnknown` and `occasionsIndex.undatedNote`. The same nine are in the `de` and `pl` drafts, and `pl` adds `shop.category.lede`. Seed copy `descriptionMd` holds them in 14 `en` rows, 2 `en-gb` rows, 5 `de` rows and 5 `pl` rows, and `content/corridors/en/pl-guide.md` holds 4 in its body.

1. **The rule.** No U+2014 EM DASH appears in any text a buyer or a florist can read:
   - every value of `messages/en.json`, `messages/en-gb.json`, `messages/de.json` and `messages/pl.json`;
   - every string field of `seed/data/copy/**` (spec 006's copy rows);
   - every file under `content/corridors/`, `content/pages/` and `content/legal/`, frontmatter and body;
   - `src/modules/i18n/error-copy.data.ts`.
   A spaced U+2013 EN DASH or a spaced hyphen used as a dash is refused too, as in spec 003 A-founder-2026-10-15a clause 2. A markdown list marker at the start of a line, hyphenated words ("hand-tied") and unspaced ranges pass. Outside the rule: `messages/admin/` (staff only), code comments, and note fields of seed data files that no page renders (`_comment`, `notes`).
2. **The replacement.** As spec 003 A-founder-2026-10-15a clause 2: a colon or a full stop takes a single dash's place; a pair of dashes around an aside becomes a pair of commas or brackets; a dash that opens a value (today's `corridor.facts.deliveryDays.none` starts with one) is dropped and the value starts with a capital ("None to state"). The words either side stay unless the sentence no longer reads. Each changed `en` value is listed in the PR, old and new.
3. **Review state.** As spec 003 A17 and A-founder-2026-10-31a: every changed `en` value ships `reviewed: false`, and so does a changed seed row's triple (spec 006). The builder writes `.claude/state/record-approval-<task>.py` with a `--check` mode that prints every text it records; the orchestrator reads it and runs it under the delegation. `de` and `pl` are machine drafts of the new English, `reviewed: false`.
4. **The check.** `pnpm i18n:check` refuses the rule's characters in every key of the four catalogues; it is an error that names the locale and the key. One unit test applies the same pattern to the seed copy, the `content/` files and `error-copy.data.ts`, naming the file and the field or line. Both read one shared pattern, extending `src/modules/i18n/head-punctuation.ts`, so the head rule and the body rule cannot drift.
5. **Keys whose owner is still open.** TASK-251's four consent keys are listed in a closed `PENDING_DASH_KEYS` with that task's id. The check skips only those keys, and it fails when a listed key no longer holds a dash, so the list only shrinks. TASK-251 deletes its entries when it lands. TASK-428's key is not listed: the cleanup task depends on TASK-428.
6. **SEO.** Body copy only; the head strings stay spec 003 A-founder-2026-10-15a's. No URL, canonical, hreflang, sitemap or indexability verdict changes.
7. **i18n.** No new key. ICU arguments do not change, so no call site changes.
8. **Compliance.** No legal, price or VAT text changes meaning. `corridor.facts.orderBy.value` states the cutoff; its `{time}` and `{zone}` arguments and the words "the recipient's own time, not yours" stay.
9. **Owner and class.** One task (TASK-430). Review-only: copy and an i18n check, no price, cutoff logic or legal text.

#### Acceptance criteria

- **AC-117 (no em dash in user-facing text).** On the committed tree, no value of the four catalogues, no string of `seed/data/copy/**`, no file under `content/corridors/`, `content/pages/` or `content/legal/`, and no string of `error-copy.data.ts` contains U+2014, a spaced U+2013 or a spaced hyphen used as a dash, apart from the keys in `PENDING_DASH_KEYS`. `pnpm i18n:check` exits non-zero, naming the locale and the key, on a fixture catalogue whose `corridor.facts.orderBy.value` is today's `en` value (an em dash after `{zone}`); the unit check fails, naming the file and the field, on a seed fixture and on a content fixture holding one em dash each. Each of the trigger's nine `en` keys holds a value with no dash, and `.claude/state/record-approval-430.py --check` prints every changed `en` value, old and new, and exits 0; no agent sets `reviewed: true`.

#### Test cases

| ID | Layer | Given / When / Then | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-123 | unit (`i18n:check` fixtures + content and seed scan) | (a) The committed tree: no hit outside `PENDING_DASH_KEYS`. (b) Fixtures: an em dash in a catalogue value, in a seed `descriptionMd`, in a guide body and in guide frontmatter; a spaced en dash; a spaced hyphen; each reported with its file or locale and its key. A list marker "- " at the start of a line, "hand-tied" and "9-17" pass. (c) A `PENDING_DASH_KEYS` entry whose value has no dash fails. Red with U+2014 removed from the pattern, red with `content/` dropped from the scan | AC-117 |
